166. Chestnut Brownie Dessert

Category: Plated Dessert | Prep: N/A | Cook/Bake: 10 mins (Brownie) + 5 mins (Almond Tuile) | Yield: 20 cm x 20 cm/ 10 portions 4 cm x 10 cm / 1700 g

Ingredients

Component Overview: Brownie Chestnut Slice is made up of 7 components as under: A) Brownie, B) Crunchy Feuilletine Layer, C) Chestnut Cream, D) Mascarpone Cream Chantilly, E) Dark Chocolate Ganache, F) Almond Tuille, G) Aerated Chocolate, H) Assembling and Presenting.

A. Brownie (20 cm x 20 cm frame, 8 portions)

  • 82 g Butter
  • 46 g Dark chocolate 55%
  • 66 g Egg, whole
  • 66 g Caster sugar
  • 26 g Glucose
  • 32 g All-purpose flour
  • 7.5 g Cocoa powder
  • 35 g Pecan nuts

B. Crunchy Feuilletine Layer

  • 30 g Milk chocolate
  • 70 g Praline paste
  • 35 g Feuilletine

C. Chestnut Cream

  • 225 g Chestnut paste
  • 150 g Cream

D. Vanilla Cream Chantilly

  • 150 g Cream
  • 20 g Sugar
  • 1 g / ¼ tsp Vanilla

E. Dark Chocolate Ganache

  • 100 g Dark chocolate 55%
  • 100 g Cream

F. Almond Tuile

  • 50 g Sugar
  • 1 g Pectin jaune
  • 18 g Milk
  • 40 g Butter
  • 18 g Glucose
  • 62 g Almond nibs

G. Aerated Chocolate

  • 200 g Milk chocolate
  • 3 pcs. Espuma gas bulbs

H. Assembling and Presenting

  • 50 g Dark chocolate (melted)
  • As required Micro greens
  • As required Aerated Chocolate

Method

A. Brownie

  1. Preheat the oven to 160° C.
  2. Line a 20 cm x 20 cm frame with baking paper and seal with aluminum foil.
  3. Sift flour and cocoa powder.
  4. In a mixing bowl, cream together butter, sugar, and glucose, until fluffy.
  5. Melt chocolate, and mix into the above mixture.
  6. Beat in eggs little at a time.
  7. Fold in the sifted flour and cocoa powder.
  8. Lastly fold in the chopped pecan nuts.
  9. Spread the mixture in 20 x 20 cm square frame and bake in the preheated oven for 10 minutes.
  10. Once baked place the tray in the freezer to cool.

B. Crunchy Feuilletine Layer

  1. Melt Milk chocolate over bain marie, mix in the praline paste, and lastly fold in the feuilletine.
  2. Spread the mixture evenly on top of the cooled brownie and place it back in the freezer to set.

C. Chestnut Cream

  1. Place chestnut paste and cold cream together in a bowl, and whisk with an electric hand blender to stiff peak and refrigerate until required.

D. Vanilla Cream Chantilly

  1. In a large mixing bowl place cream, sugar and vanilla and whip it to stiff peak.
  2. Refrigerate until required.

E. Dark Chocolate Ganache

  1. Line an 11 cm x 11 cm square frame with baking paper and seal with aluminum foil.
  2. Place dark chocolate in a mixing bowl.
  3. Boil cream and pour over the chocolate and mix to form a ganache.
  4. Pour the ganache into the prepared frame and set it in the freezer.

F. Almond Tuile

  1. Preheat the oven to 160° C.
  2. Mix sugar and pectin jaune powder.
  3. Bring milk, butter, and glucose to a boil.
  4. Add in the sugar and pectin mixture, followed by the almond nibs.
  5. Spread the mixture in between 2 sheets of baking paper and roll to 1 – 2 mm thin, and place it in the freezer to set.
  6. When firm, peel off one layer of baking paper, and bake in the preheated oven for 5 min or till golden brown and cut into desired shapes when warm.

G. Aerated Chocolate

  1. Warm the espuma bottle in an oven at 30° C.
  2. Melt the chocolate and pour into the espuma bottle.
  3. Fill the bottle with 3 gas bulbs; seal and shake well before foaming.
  4. Press out the chocolate foam into a deep container and place in the freezer to harden.

H. Assembling and Presenting

  1. Fill the chestnut cream and Mascarpone Cream Chantilly in separate piping bags with 7 mm plain nozzles and alternatively pipe onto the brownie base in neat parallel lines, to cover the surface.
  2. Freeze and cut the Brownie in to cm x 4 cm [Note: 10 cm x 4 cm per Yield header] portions, using a chef’s knife and hot water to warm the blade.
  3. Similarly cut the set ganache into 2 x 2 cm cubes and keep aside.
  4. Cut the almond tuile 2 x 2 cm pieces.
  5. Place each portion of the brownie in the centre of a round dessert plate.
  6. Place the ganache cubes on each end.
  7. Place almond tuile on top of each ganache cube.
  8. Break the aerated chocolate garnish into pieces and use decoratively.
  9. Finish with chocolate garnishes.
  10. Keep refrigerated until served.

Chef’s Notes & Technical Parameters

  • Baking / Cooking Temp: Bake Brownie at 160°C (320°F) for 10 minutes; Bake Almond Tuile at 160°C (320°F) for 5 minutes; Warm espuma bottle in oven at 30°C (86°F).
  • Target Texture / Visual Doneness: Butter, sugar, and glucose creamed until fluffy; Chestnut Cream and Vanilla Cream Chantilly whipped to stiff peak; Almond Tuile rolled 1–2 mm thin and baked until golden brown.
  • Storage / Shelf Life: Freeze brownie, feuilletine layer, ganache, and aerated chocolate to set/harden; keep assembled plates refrigerated until served.
  • Additional Notes & Equipment:
    • Equipment: Measuring scale, Measuring spoon set, Mixing bowls, White chopping board, Chef knife, Electric hand mixer, Sauce pan, Rubber spatula, Baking frame 20 cm x 20 cm, Baking paper, Aluminum foil, Baking tray, Scissors, Blender, Espuma bottle, Palette knife, Oven mitts/gloves, Piping bag, Piping nozzle, 7 mm, plain, Cooling rack, Paring knife/ bamboo skewer.
    • Key Learning: Chopping, Melting, Creaming method, Folding, Baking, Whipping, Boiling, Aerating (using espuma bottle), Assembling and presenting the dessert.
    • Notes:
      1. To multiply the recipe, multiply all ingredients proportionately.
↑ Back to top

167. Coconut - Passion Fruit Dome

Category: Plated Dessert / Entremet | Prep: 10–15 mins lemongrass infusion + 30–60 mins blast freeze | Cook/Bake: 10–12 mins (Daquoise) + 6–7 mins (Macaroon Drops) + 20–30 secs microwave (Sponge) | Yield: N/A

Ingredients

Component Overview: Coconut- Passion Fruit Dome is made up of 6 components as under: A) Coconut Daquoise, B) Passion fruit Lemongrass mousse, C) Passion fruit glaze, D) White Chocolate Chantilly, E) Coconut Macaroon Drops, F) Microwave Sponge, G) Finishing, H) Assembling and Presenting.

A. Coconut Daquoise

  • 50 g Egg white
  • 20 g Caster sugar
  • 1 pinch Crème of tartar
  • 40 g Desiccated coconut
  • 40 g Icing sugar
  • 8 g Flour

B. Passion fruit lemongrass mousse

  • 20 g Egg yolk
  • 50 g Sugar
  • 1 pcs Gelatin Leaves
  • 100 g Passion fruit puree
  • ½ pcs Lemongrass
  • 150 g Cream

C. Passion fruit Glaze

  • 100 g Neutral glaze
  • 50 g Passion fruit puree
  • 1.5 pcs Gelatin leaves
  • A/R Yellow coloring

D. White Chocolate Chantilly

  • 50 g White chocolate
  • 25 g Cream I.
  • 75 g Cream II.

E. Coconut Macaroon Drops

  • 10 g Desiccated coconut
  • 10 g Sugar
  • 10 g Egg white

F. Microwave Sponge

  • 1 pc Egg
  • 12.5 g Sugar
  • 30 g Glucose
  • 25 g Flour
  • 5 g Baking Powder
  • A/R Colour
  • ½ tsp Vanilla

G. Assembling and Finishing

  • A/R Roasted dessicated coconut
  • 50 g Dark chocolate (melted)
  • As required Micro greens
  • As required Aerated Chocolate

Method

A. Coconut Daquoise

  1. Sieve icing sugar and flour together and mix with coconut.
  2. Make French meringue from egg white, crème of tartar and caster sugar.
  3. Fold dry ingredients to the meringue and fold just until combined.
  4. Spread mixture on to a lined baking tray and bake at 160°C for 10-12 minutes.

B. Passion fruit lemongrass mousse

  1. Soak gelatin in ice-cold water and drain it when soft.
  2. Make sabayon from egg yolk and sugar.
  3. Boil passion fruit with crushed lemon grass; take off the heat and cover to infuse for 10-15 minutes.
  4. Remove lemon grass, boil the puree again, take off the heat and add gelatin.
  5. Mix until smooth and pour in mixing bowl.
  6. Whip Cream to soft peak stage.
  7. When fruit puree lukewarm, add sabayon.
  8. Fold in whipped cream in 2 additions.

C. Passion fruit Glaze

  1. Soak and drain gelatin.
  2. Heat fruit puree in a saucepan until melts.
  3. Add neutral glaze and heat until warm.
  4. Add gelatin and mix until smooth.

D. White Chocolate Chantilly

  1. Make ganache from white chocolate and cream I.
  2. Whip cream II. and fold in the ganache in 2 additions.
  3. Keep in the fridge.

E. Coconut Macaroon Drops

  1. Mix all ingredients together until it forms a paste.
  2. Make little drops on the silicon mat and bake at 160°C for 6-7 minutes or until golden brown.

F. Microwave Sponge

  1. In a mixing bowl, whip the eggs, sugar, glucose, colour and vanilla till the mixture doubles in volume.
  2. Sift the flour and baking powder and gently fold it into the egg mixture.
  3. Coat a 3oz paper cup with oil and pierce the bottom of the cup with 3 – 4 holes, using a skewer or toothpick.
  4. Fill the paper cups half way, and microwave it for 20 – 30 seconds, or until cooked.
  5. Allow the sponge to cool, in the cup, upside down.

G. Assembling and Finishing

  1. Cut daquoise with round plain cutter, 1 large and 1 small size for each cake.
  2. Pipe mousse on bottom of dome molds, place smaller ring on it.
  3. Repeat step 1, one more time. Larger disc close the top.
  4. Blast freeze for about 30-60 minutes.
  5. Remold and transfer domes to chiller.
  6. When defrosted, place them on grids and pour the lukewarm glaze over the top.
  7. Let the excess drop and transfer cake on a tray, roasted desiccated coconut on it.
  8. Shake cake on the coconut to make coconut stick on the bottom. Transfer it to a clean tray.
  9. Place cake on plate.
  10. Place 1 dark chocolate ring on top.
  11. Place a white chocolate Chantilly canella on top.
  12. Garnish the plate with drops of glaze and coconut macaroons.
  13. Serve immediately.

Chef’s Notes & Technical Parameters

  • Baking / Cooking Temp: Bake Coconut Daquoise at 160°C (320°F) for 10–12 minutes; Bake Coconut Macaroon Drops at 160°C (320°F) for 6–7 minutes; Microwave Sponge for 20–30 seconds.
  • Target Texture / Visual Doneness: Mousse cream whipped to soft peak stage; Coconut Macaroon Drops baked until golden brown; Microwave sponge batter whipped until doubled in volume and cooled upside down in cup; lukewarm glaze poured over defrosted domes.
  • Storage / Shelf Life: Keep White Chocolate Chantilly in fridge; blast freeze domes for 30–60 minutes, remold and transfer to chiller; serve immediately after plating.
  • Additional Notes & Equipment:
    • Equipment: Measuring scale, Measuring spoon set, Mixing bowls, White chopping board, Chef knife, Electric hand mixer, Sauce pan, Rubber spatula, Silicon Mold, semi sphere, Baking paper, Baking tray, Scissors, Palette knife, Oven mitts/gloves, Piping bag, Cooling rack, Paring knife/ bamboo skewer.
    • Key Learning: Making a Microwave Sponge, Making a Daquoise, Glazing, Folding, Baking, Whipping, Assembling and presenting the dessert.
    • Notes:
      1. To multiply the recipe, multiply all ingredients proportionately.

168. Fruit 4 ways

Category: Plated Dessert | Prep: N/A | Cook/Bake: 2–3 mins (Tuile Disc) + 2 hrs sous vide (Melon) + 2 hrs dehydrator (Fruit) | Yield: N/A

Ingredients

Component Overview: This plated dessert is made up of 5 components as under: A) Tuile Disc, B) Fruit Soup, C) Granita, D) Compressed brulee melon, E) Dehydrated Fruit.

A. Tuile Disc

  • 35 g Egg white
  • 35 g Icing sugar
  • 30 g Flour
  • 25 g Butter

B. Fruit Soup

  • 250 g Canned apricot
  • 40 g Sugar
  • 40 g Crème fraiche

C. Granita

  • 200 g Rock melon (cleaned)
  • 20 g Sugar
  • 100 g Water
  • 1 pcs Gelatin Leaves bronze

D. Compressed bruleed melon

  • 300 g Rock Melon
  • 1 pcs Star Anise

E. Dehydrated Fruit

  • 1 pcs Kiwi
  • 4 pcs Strawberries
  • 1 pcs Apple

Method

A. Tuile Disc

  1. Sieve icing sugar and flour together.
  2. Add egg white, mix until smooth and mix in melted butter.
  3. Spread immediately with stencil (2 pcs, 140 mm diameter).
  4. Bake them at 160°C for 2-3 minutes or until the side turns to golden color.
  5. Cut in the middle immediately after baking with a 100 mm diameter plain cutter.
  6. Keep in a dry and cool place until serving.

B. Fruit Soup

  1. Drain apricot and blend with sugar and crème fraiche until smooth and creamy.
  2. Keep in the chiller until serving.

C. Granita

  1. Soak gelatin in the ice-cold water.
  2. Cut peeled rock melon to small cubes and boil with sugar.
  3. Take off the heat and add gelatin with water.
  4. Strain and pour into a lined high edge tray in a thin layer and blast freeze.
  5. Blend it before serving.

D. Compressed bruleed melon

  1. Cut peeled rock melon to larger pieces and place them in sous vide pack with a piece of star anise.
  2. Vacuum seal and sous vide it for 2 hours on 65°C. Keep them in ice cold water.

E. Dehydrated Fruit

  1. Cut fruits to thin slices and place them in the dehydrator.
  2. Dry them for 2 hours on 57°C.

F. Assembling and Finishing

  1. Pipe some soup into a deep plate.
  2. Place the tuille disc on it on the way it does not touch the soup.
  3. Place compressed fruit in and the dried fruits around the soup, on the tuille.
  4. Place 3 spoons of granita on the frame of the plate just before plating. Serve immediately.

Chef’s Notes & Technical Parameters

  • Baking / Cooking Temp: Bake Tuile Disc at 160°C (320°F) for 2–3 minutes; Sous vide Compressed Melon at 65°C (149°F) for 2 hours; Dehydrate Fruit at 57°C (134.6°F) for 2 hours.
  • Target Texture / Visual Doneness: Tuile baked until sides turn golden color and cut immediately with a 100 mm cutter; Fruit soup blended until smooth and creamy; Granita blast frozen in a thin layer and blended before serving.
  • Storage / Shelf Life: Keep Tuile Disc in a dry and cool place until serving; keep Fruit Soup in chiller until serving; keep sous-vide melon in ice-cold water; serve plated dessert immediately.
  • Additional Notes & Equipment:
    • Equipment: Measuring scale, Measuring spoon set, Mixing bowls, White chopping board, Chef knife, Dehydrator, Sauce pan, Rubber spatula, Baking paper, Baking tray, Scissors, Blender, Vacuum bags, Palette knife, Oven mitts/gloves, Paring knife/ bamboo skewer.
    • Key Learning: Making a Granita, Infusion through compression, Dehydrating fruit.
    • Notes:
      1. To multiply the recipe, multiply all ingredients proportionately.
↑ Back to top

169. Set Cardamom Rice Pudding

Category: Plated Dessert | Prep: 30 mins rice soaking + 10–15 mins saffron infusion | Cook/Bake: 8–10 mins (Filo Layer) | Yield: N/A

Ingredients

Component Overview: This plated dessert is made up of 6 components as under: A. Rice Pudding, B. Rice Crispy base, C. White Chocolate Glaze, D. Caramelized Pineapple, E. Saffron Pastry cream, F. Filo Layer, G. Coral Tuile, H. Assembling and Finishing.

A. Rice Pudding

  • 150 g Milk
  • 200 g Coconut Milk
  • 35 g Light Brown Sugar
  • 2 pcs Cardamom Seeds
  • 35 g Short Grain Rice
  • 20 g White Chocolate
  • 1 pcs Gelatin Leaves
  • 125 g Whipping Cream

B. Rice Crispy Base

  • 22 g White Chocolate
  • 12 g Cocoa Butter
  • 20 g Rice Crispy
  • 12 g Pistachio Nibs
  • 1 tsp Praline Paste

C. White Chocolate Glaze

  • 67 g Cream
  • 25 g Sugar
  • 12 g Glucose
  • 25 g White Chocolate
  • 2/3 pcs Gelatin Leaves

D. Caramelized Pineapple

  • 50 g Sugar
  • 10 g Butter
  • 1 pcs Star anise
  • 1 pcs Clove
  • ½ pcs Cinnamon stick
  • 1 tsp Lemon juice
  • 60 g (2 slices) Pineapple, fresh

E. Saffron Pastry Cream

  • 75 ml Milk
  • 75 ml Cream
  • 35 g Sugar
  • 1 pcs Egg yolk
  • 10 g Corn flour
  • Few strands Saffron
  • 5 g Butter

F. Filo Layer

  • 2 sheets Filo Pastry Sheet
  • A/R Butter, Melted

G. Coral Tuile

  • 65 g Oil
  • 55 g Water
  • 10 g Flour
  • A pinch Salt

H. Assembling and Finishing

  • A/R Fresh fruits/ berries
  • A/R Pineapple sauce

Method

A. Rice Pudding

  1. Soak rice in hot water for 30 minutes.
  2. Soak gelatin to ice cold water.
  3. Boil milk, coconut milk and light brown sugar.
  4. Once boiled, add drained rice and cook on low heat.
  5. When rice is half cooked, add crushed cardamom seeds. Keep stirring constantly.
  6. Once rice is soft and the pudding is done, take off the heat and add drained gelatin.
  7. Place white chocolate (chopped or chips) into a clean mixing bowl and pour the rice pudding over.
  8. Mix it, cling film contact and allow to cool down.
  9. Whip the cream and fold into the cold rice.
  10. Fill the mixture into piping bag with no nozzle and pipe it into medium size dome silicon molds.
  11. Allow them to set in the freezer.
  12. Remold once they are frozen.
  13. Glaze them with white chocolate glaze before plating.

B. Rice Crispy Base

  1. Melt cocoa butter in a sauce pan and pour it over the white chocolate.
  2. Add rice crisps and pistachio nibs and mix well.
  3. Spread it on a silicon mat sheet an place in the chiller to set.

C. White Chocolate Glaze

  1. Soak gelatin in ice cold water.
  2. Boil cream, glucose, sugar together.
  3. Take off the heat and add drained gelatin.
  4. Mix until melted and pour it over the white chocolate.

D. Caramelized Pineapple

  1. Cut pineapple to small segments.
  2. Caramelize sugar in a sauce pan.
  3. Add lemon juice and butter.
  4. Add the pineapple and spices.
  5. Cook slowly until the majority of water evaporates.

E. Saffron Pastry Cream

  1. Mix the milk, cream and saffron together and bring ¾ of the mixture to a boil in a saucepan. Take off the heat and allow the saffron to infuse for 10 – 15 minutes.
  2. Pour the hot mixture on the egg yolk- sugar mixture, whisking continuously.
  3. Pour it back in the saucepan and bring it to boil, while continuously mixing.
  4. Cook until it bubbles right in the center and pour into a clean mixing bowl.
  5. Keep whisking until it is lukewarm and then mix in butter.

F. Filo Layer

  1. Brush half a sheet of filo pastry with melted butter and fold up to 40mm wide.
  2. Cut folded pastry lines into 80mm long pieces.
  3. Place them into silicon sheets and bake at 160°C for 8-10mins.

G. Coral Tuile

  1. In a mixing bowl, mix all the ingredients and add food colour if desired.
  2. Heat up a non stick pan and pour in a little vegetable oil, just enough to coat the bottom of the pan.
  3. Mix the coral tuile mixture well and pour enough for one tuile ( about 30 – 60 ml) into the pan and allow for it to crisp up.
  4. With the help of a skewer, release the tuile from the pan and place on a paper towel to remove any excess oil.

H. Assembling and Finishing

  1. Cut rice crispy base with round cutter and place it onto an oval plate. Place rice pudding dome on it.
  2. Place 1 filo layer next to it and pipe the saffron pastry cream on it in 3 spots. Fill up the gaps with the caramelized pineapple.
  3. Repeat it 1 more time and cover with a third filo layer.
  4. Drizzle the pineapple sauce around.
  5. Decorate with mixed berries and the coral tuile.

Chef’s Notes & Technical Parameters

  • Baking / Cooking Temp: Bake Filo Layer at 160°C (320°F) for 8–10 minutes; Cook Rice Pudding on low heat after boiling; Pan-fry Coral Tuile in a heated non-stick pan.
  • Target Texture / Visual Doneness: Rice cooked until soft; Saffron Pastry Cream cooked until bubbling right in the center; Coral Tuile cooked until crisped up and drained on paper towel.
  • Storage / Shelf Life: Cool rice pudding with contact cling film before folding in whipped cream; freeze rice pudding domes in silicon molds before remolding and glazing; set rice crispy base in chiller.
  • Additional Notes & Equipment:
    • Equipment: Measuring scale, Measuring spoon set, Mixing bowls, White chopping board, Chef knife, Electric hand mixer, Sauce pan, Non stick pan, Rubber spatula, Baking paper, Baking tray, Scissors, Blender, Palette knife, Oven mitts/gloves, Piping bag, Piping nozzle, 7 mm, plain, Cooling rack, Paring knife/ bamboo skewer.
    • Key Learning: Chopping, Folding, Baking, Whipping, Boiling, Making infused cream patisserie, Assembling and presenting the dessert.
    • Notes:
      1. To multiply the recipe, multiply all ingredients proportionately.
